...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 ot the constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate writ, order or Direction more particularly in the
nature of Writ of Mandamus declaring the action of the respondents in not paying
the compensation and other statuary benefits to the petitioners in pursuance of
acquiring their land, houses cattle, cattle sheds and labour for the purse of
establishmenUconstruction of Sripada Sagar (Yellampally) project (SSyp), as
discriminatory, defamatory, illegal, arbitrary, un-constitutional besides being
violative of the fundamental rights guaranteed by the Constitution of lndia to the
woman like the petitioners who all are sufferers of the land acquisition and also
official discrimination and bias for the sole reasoh that the petitioners are woman
and need not be considered as eligible for getting welfare, statutory benefits and
compensatory wages on par with the men, and consequentially to direct the 2nd
and 3rd respondents to pay forthwith the due compensation and other statuary
benefits in terms of G.O.Ms. No. 68 lrrigation and CAD (PW) Dept. dated 08-04-
2005 and the consequential amendment of G.O.Ms. No. 88 dt. 26-08-2010 to the
petitioners otherwise the petitioners will suffer serious loss and hardship.

l.A. NO: 1 OF 2014(WPMP. NO: 10973 OF 2014
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondents to stop proceeding with the construction process of the Sripada
Sagar (Yellampally) Project (SSYP), pending disposal of the above Writ Petition
Counsel for the Petitioner Nos.1 to 215: SRl. M GIRI
Counsel for the Petitioner Nos.216 to .1351 : SRI KIRAN PALAKURTHI
Counsel for the Respondents: GP FOR LAND ACQUISITION
The Court made the fo owing: ORDER
)

HON'BLE MRS JUSTICE SUREPALLI NANDA
WRIT PETITION 0.8789 0F 20L4
ORDER
Heard learned counsel for the petitioners and
learned Government Pleader for Land Acquisition
appearing for respondents.
2. The petitioners approached the Court seeking the
prayer as follows:
"To issue an appropriate Writ Order or
Direction more particularly in the nature of Writ of
Mandamus declaring the action of the respondents
in not paying the compensation and other statutory
benefits to the petitioners in pursuance oF acquiring
their land, houses cattle, cattle sheds and labour for the purpose of esta blish men t/construction of
Sripada Sagar (Yellampally) project (SSyp), as
discriminatory defamatory illegal a rbitra ry
u nconstitu tiona I besides being violative of the
fundamental rights guaranteed by the Constitution
of India to the woman like the petitioners who all
are sufferers of the Iand acquisition and also official
discrimination and bias for the sole reason that the
petitioners are woman and need not be considered
as eligible for getting welfare, statutory benefits and
compensatory wages on par with the men and
consequentially to direct the 2nd and 3rd
respondents to pay forthwith the due compensation
and other statuary benefits in terms of
G.O.Ms.No.68 Irrigation and CAD (PW) Dept dated
08-04-2005 and the consequential amendment of
J--_,,..-.)

2
G.O.t"ls.No.BB dt 26.08.2010 to the petitioners
other,.vise the petit.ioners will suffer serious loss and
ha rdsh ip. "
3. The petitioners are residents of Karnamamidi,
Kondapally, Padthanpally, villages of Mancherial
(Mandal) Gullakota village of Luxettipet (Mandal)
Distrcit Adilabad, they all are major daughters
belonging to the agirucltural families of the above
mentioned four villages. Being aggrieved by the action
of the respondents in showing Gender/Sex
.discrimination against the applicants in paying
compensation and loss of wages to the petitioners who
lost their lands, houses, cattle sheds etc., which are
acquired for the purpose of construction of .SSYP
Sripada Sagar (Yellampally) Project in the manner and
on par with the men in their respective villages, in utter
violence and contrarY to the provisions of G.O.Ms.No.68
Irrigation and CAD (Pw) Department dated 08.04.2OO5,
and the consequential amendments to it vide
G.O.Ms.No.88 dated 26.08.2010, the present writ
petition is filed,

4. Learned counsel appearing on behalf of the
petitioners brings on record the order of this Court
dated 14.03.2022 passed in W.P.No.L422O of 2O2O and
contended that the petitioners herein are entitled for
similar relief. The relevant portion of the said order
para 15 reads as under:
"For the afore-stated reasons, the writ petition is
allowed. The official respondents are directed to conduct
a survey and identify the daughters whose parents'
la nds/structu res were affected in the project. It is
needless to observe that the parameters For extending
the benefit for the daught€r should be that should be a
major as on the date of notification under Section 4(1),
that she should be dependent on the head of the family
and should be residing in that family as on the date of
notification. After identification of the daughters, who
meet the above criterion, the authorities concerned are
directed to see that the benefits of the package, which
have been extended to the sons in the project affected
families and project displaced persons, are also to be
extended to the major daughters on par with the major
sons, as per the terms and conditions of G.O.M5.No.B8,
dated 26.08.2010. The entire exercise of conducting the
survey and extending the benefits shall be completed,
as expeditiously as possible, preferably within a period
of six months from the date of receipt of copy of this
order".
3

4
5. It is represented by learned Government Pleader
for Land Acquisition that the writ petition could be
disposed of by granting the identical prayer as ordered
at paragraph No.15 in W.P.No.14220 of 2O2O dated
L4.O3.2(J22 (referred to and extracted above).
6. Taking into consideration the facts and circumstances of
the case and the submissions of learned counsel for the
petitioners and learnr:c Government Pleader for Land
Acqursitoin, the writ petition is allowed directing the official
iespondents to conducl a survey and identity the daughters,
whose parents'lands/structures were affected in the Sripada
sagar (Yellampally) Pro;ect (SSYP) in the four villages of the
petitioners i.e., Karnanramidi. Kondapally, t']adthanpally,
villages of t'lancherial (Mandal) Gullakota village of Luxettipet
(Mandal) Distrcit Adilabad. It is needless to observe that the
parameters for extending the benefit for the daughter should
be that she should be a major as on the date of notification
under Section 4(f), that she should be dependent on the head
of the family and should be residing in that family as on the
date of notification. Afte,r identification of the daughters, who
met the above criterior), the authorities concerned are

directed to see that the benefits of the package, which have
been extended to the sons in the project aFfected families and
project displaced persons, are also to be extended to the
major daughters on par with the major sons, as per the terms
and conditions of G.O.Ms.No.BB, dated 26.08.2010. The entire
exercise of conducting survey and extending the benefits shall
be completed, as expeditiously as possible, preferably within a
period of six (06) months from the date of receipt of copy of
this order. However, there shall be no order as to costs.
Miscellaneous petitions, if any, pending in this Writ
Petition, shall stand closed.
